FROM seekers AS s
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT s0.guid, s0."SeekerId", s0."SkillId", s1.guid AS guid0, s1."Name"
    FROM "seekerSkills" AS s0
    INNER JOIN skills AS s1 ON s0."SkillId" = s1.guid
) AS s2 ON s.guid = s2."SeekerId"

) AS s3 ON s.guid = s3."SeekerId"

FROM seekers AS s
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT s0.guid, s0."SeekerId", s0."SkillId", s1.guid AS guid0, s1."Name"
    FROM "seekerSkills" AS s0
    INNER JOIN skills AS s1 ON s0."SkillId" = s1.guid
) AS s2 ON s.guid = s2."SeekerId"
LEFT JOIN educations AS e ON s.guid = e."SeekerId"
LEFT JOIN experiences AS e0 ON s.guid = e0."SeekerId"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT j.guid, j."AppliedOn", j."IsDeleted", j."JobPostId", j."SeekerId", j."Status", j0.guid AS guid0, j0."Description", j0."EmploymentPosition", j0."EmploymentType", j0."IsDeleted" AS "IsDeleted0", j0."LastDate", j0."Location", j0."PostedDate", j0."RecruiterID", j0."SalaryPackage", j0."Title"

    INNER JOIN "jobPosts" AS j0 ON j."JobPostId" = j0.guid
) AS s3 ON s.guid = s3."SeekerId"
WHERE NOT (s."IsDeleted")
ORDER BY s.guid, s2.guid, s2.guid0, e.guid, e0."Guid", s3.guid

SELECT s.guid, s."About", s."ConnectionId", s."DateOfBirth", s."FirstName", s."Gender", s."IsDeleted", s."LastName", s."UserId", s2.guid, s2."SeekerId", s2."SkillId", s2.guid0, s2."Name", e.guid, e."SeekerId", e."collegeName", e."courseName", e.grade, e0."Guid", e0."CompanyName", e0."Description", e0."FromMonth", e0."FromYear", e0."Role", e0."SeekerId", e0."ToMonth", e0."ToYear", s3.guid, s3."AppliedOn", s3."IsDeleted", s3."JobPostId", s3."SeekerId", s3."Status", s3.guid0, s3."Description", s3."EmploymentPosition", s3."EmploymentType", s3."IsDeleted0", s3."LastDate", s3."Location", s3."PostedDate", s3."RecruiterID", s3."SalaryPackage", s3."Title"
FROM seekers AS s
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT s0.guid, s0."SeekerId", s0."SkillId", s1.guid AS guid0, s1."Name"
    FROM "seekerSkills" AS s0
    INNER JOIN skills AS s1 ON s0."SkillId" = s1.guid
) AS s2 ON s.guid = s2."SeekerId"
LEFT JOIN educations AS e ON s.guid = e."SeekerId"
LEFT JOIN experiences AS e0 ON s.guid = e0."SeekerId"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT j.guid, j."AppliedOn", j."IsDeleted", j."JobPostId", j."SeekerId", j."Status", j0.guid AS guid0, j0."Description", j0."EmploymentPosition", j0."EmploymentType", j0."IsDeleted" AS "IsDeleted0", j0."LastDate", j0."Location", j0."PostedDate", j0."RecruiterID", j0."SalaryPackage", j0."Title"
    FROM "jobApplications" AS j
    INNER JOIN "jobPosts" AS j0 ON j."JobPostId" = j0.guid
) AS s3 ON s.guid = s3."SeekerId"
WHERE NOT (s."IsDeleted")
ORDER BY s.guid, s2.guid, s2.guid0, e.guid, e0."Guid", s3.guid

SELECT s.guid, s."About", s."ConnectionId", s."DateOfBirth", s."FirstName", s."Gender", s."IsDeleted", s."LastName", s."UserId", s2.guid, s2."SeekerId", s2."SkillId", s2.guid0, s2."Name", e.guid, e."SeekerId", e."collegeName", e."courseName", e.grade, e0."Guid", e0."CompanyName", e0."Description", e0."FromMonth", e0."FromYear", e0."Role", e0."SeekerId", e0."ToMonth", e0."ToYear", s3.guid, s3."AppliedOn", s3."IsDeleted", s3."JobPostId", s3."SeekerId", s3."Status", s3.guid0, s3."Description", s3."EmploymentPosition", s3."EmploymentType", s3."IsDeleted0", s3."LastDate", s3."Location", s3."PostedDate", s3."RecruiterID", s3."SalaryPackage", s3."Title"
FROM seekers AS s
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT s0.guid, s0."SeekerId", s0."SkillId", s1.guid AS guid0, s1."Name"
    FROM "seekerSkills" AS s0
    INNER JOIN skills AS s1 ON s0."SkillId" = s1.guid
) AS s2 ON s.guid = s2."SeekerId"
LEFT JOIN educations AS e ON s.guid = e."SeekerId"
LEFT JOIN experiences AS e0 ON s.guid = e0."SeekerId"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT j.guid, j."AppliedOn", j."IsDeleted", j."JobPostId", j."SeekerId", j."Status", j0.guid AS guid0, j0."Description", j0."EmploymentPosition", j0."EmploymentType", j0."IsDeleted" AS "IsDeleted0", j0."LastDate", j0."Location", j0."PostedDate", j0."RecruiterID", j0."SalaryPackage", j0."Title"
    FROM "jobApplications" AS j
    INNER JOIN "jobPosts" AS j0 ON j."JobPostId" = j0.guid
) AS s3 ON s.guid = s3."SeekerId"
WHERE NOT (s."IsDeleted")
ORDER BY s.guid, s2.guid, s2.guid0, e.guid, e0."Guid", s3.guid
